            <span data-view-component="true">
  <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy link" for="issue-6254778533-permalink" role="menuitem" data-dd-action-name="pr.description.copy_link" data-view-component="true" class="dropdown-item btn-link">
      
              Copy link

</clipboard-copy>  <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true" class="sr-only" data-clipboard-copy-feedback></div>
</span>            <span data-view-component="true">
  <clipboard-copy role="menuitem" data-dd-action-name="pr.description.copy_markdown" value="## Summary

Fixes #9228. Desktop executable lookup now skips a symlink whose target is a directory and continues through PATH, matching the program a real launch selects. An explicit directory symlink returns the existing executable-not-found refusal.

## Problem and expected behavior

`access(X_OK)` follows a symbolic link, but the following `lstat().isDirectory()` inspected the link itself. A searchable directory therefore qualified when reached through a symlink. Application inventory could record that directory as `executable.resolved_path` while launching the genuine executable later on PATH. The same lookup admitted a directory symlink as xprop and failed later with `display_unreadable` instead of refusing the missing executable.

## Change and scope

Use `stat` for the type check so it inspects the same referent as the executable-access check and actual launch. Keep the selected path spelling, argv, PATH order, relative-PATH authority and existing refusals. Both inventory and xprop admission consume the shared lookup; no consumer-specific workaround is added.

## Contract and boundary impact

- Semantic owner and earliest changed stage: `ProgramLookup.isExecutable`, consumed by `inspectLinuxApplication` and `checkDesktopCaptureHost` before capture side effects.
- Contribution scope fit and linked maintainer decision: ordinary correction to existing filesystem admission; new capability scope decision not applicable.
- CLI and MCP/tool-catalog contract: existing executable-selection and missing-executable outcomes; no new inputs or result fields.
- Provider, bridge, target-format, or platform compatibility: valid executable symlinks remain supported. Directory symlinks now behave like plain directories. This does not add a desktop platform.
- Evidence, artifact, provenance, or reconstruction contract: retain the actual selected executable path and its resolved target in inventory, instead of a rejected PATH candidate&#39;s directory.
- Target selection, authority, upstream behavior, and cleanup: preserve caller command/arguments, PATH precedence and the restriction on ambient relative PATH entries. Reject invalid xprop selection before creating output.
- Generated metadata, package, or installation impact: none.

The added tests read/write temporary fixture files and launch an owned shell executable through a symlink as an independent PATH-selection control. No network services, target injection or new execution authority.

## Evidence and regression coverage

Against main `ac23dc8077e429bd61030c5d443580e432ae619b`, the two affected suites with the current owned-launcher cases produced **5 failures, 17 passes, 11 Linux-conditioned skips**:

- A directory symlink ahead of a valid executable link produced the wrong inventory despite the actual PATH launch printing the expected output.
- PATH-only, absolute and relative directory-symlink selections were incorrectly accepted.
- Directory-symlink xprop failed later as `display_unreadable` instead of the existing `executable_missing` host refusal.

With the repair, all these cases pass. Neighboring plain-directory and dangling-link PATH candidates are skipped; the selected executable symlink&#39;s original path, resolved target and argv are retained. Existing desktop-entry, env-prefix, relative/empty PATH, symlink/.. path, package-inventory, refusal, pre-launch cancellation and output cleanup cases also pass.

## Validation performed

Executed on macOS arm64 with Node 24.22.1/npm 11.16.0:

- `npm run test:focused -- src/desktop/LinuxApplicationInventory.test.ts src/application/desktop/DesktopApplicationCapture.test.ts src/desktop/DebianPackageInventory.test.ts` \u2014 **46 passed, 11 skipped**.
- `npm run check` \u2014 passed (typecheck, lint, format, Knip, metadata/module/architecture guards, production reachability report and typed lint; 0 typed-lint errors, existing warnings reported).
- `git diff --cached --check`, exact staged-byte review, credential/binary/size checks \u2014 passed.

The initial full check caught a test-group line limit; the new lookup cases were moved to their own describe group. Knip also treated the literal test-generated program name as an external binary; the launch now derives the program name from its fixture path. No checker exceptions or weakened assertions were added.

## Hosted CI follow-up

The first Linux run on `d73eb4679` failed three newly added independent launch assertions: the selected symlink to the runner&#39;s `/bin/echo` exited 1 on Ubuntu 26.04. The source suite reported 3 failures, 24,522 passes and 16 skips; Portable tests aggregated those same failures. That is a failed run, not baseline or passing evidence. The exact reason for the system echo&#39;s exit was not captured by the old assertion.

Follow-up `4d0ef65b6` replaces the host echo dependency with an owned executable shell fixture (`/bin/sh` and builtin `printf`), keeping the actual PATH launch, executable symlink, output, resolved-target and argv assertions. Failed launches now report stderr. The unchanged production repair plus the revised fixture was rerun locally: baseline **5 failed / 17 passed / 11 skipped**, candidate **46 passed / 11 skipped**, and `npm run check` passed. Current Linux CI must confirm the hosted result; no local Linux pass is claimed (the available VM failed with an I/O error before running the probe).

## Compatibility, safety, and release

No dependencies, metadata or migration changes. Full Linux/X11 capture was not run locally: the 11 Linux-only cases remain explicit skips. The host-admission test injects the supported platform but executes the real filesystem lookup and refuses before display or application launch; it does not establish a real Linux display session. Production inventory and independent local PATH launches were executed on macOS. Windows was not run, and POSIX symlink tests remain platform-gated. No stronger protection against concurrent file replacement is claimed.

## Author readiness

Human owner: @1052326311

- [ ] I reviewed the final diff and relevant existing code, understand the change and its edge cases, and take responsibility for the complete PR, including AI-assisted work.
- [ ] I checked the evidence above, confirmed it supports the current change, and recorded meaningful validation gaps.
- [ ] I declare this PR ready for initial maintainer review.

Draft pending human readiness under [CONTRIBUTING](https://github.com/morluto/rea/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md#pull-request-ownership-and-readiness).

## Before merge

- [x] Concrete issue linked; one focused outcome.
- [x] Existing contract and authority preserved; no new capability scope decision needed.
- [x] Failure cases and neighboring valid inputs covered.
- [x] No documentation, public schema or generated metadata change required.
- [x] Final diff inspected for secrets, unrelated cleanup and unsupported claims.
- [ ] Human ownership and initial readiness declarations completed.
